- **tdigest.add**: Adds one or more observations to a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.add.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.byrank**: Returns, for each input rank, a floating-point estimation of the value with that rank ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.byrank.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.byrevrank**: Returns, for each input reverse rank, an estimation of the floating-point value with that reverse rank ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.byrevrank.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.cdf**: Returns, for each input value, an estimation of the floating-point fraction of (observations smaller than the given value + half the observations equal to the given value) ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.cdf.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.create**: Allocates memory and initializes a new t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.create.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.info**: Returns information and statistics about a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.info.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.max**: Returns the maximum observation value from a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.max.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.merge**: Merges multiple t-digest sketches into a single sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.merge.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.min**: Returns the minimum observation value from a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.min.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.quantile**: Returns, for each input fraction, a floating-point estimation of the value that is smaller than the given fraction of observations ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.quantile.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.rank**: Returns, for each floating-point input value, the estimated rank of the value (the number of observations in the sketch that are smaller than the value + half the number of observations that are equal to the value) ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.rank.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.reset**: Resets a t-digest sketch (empties the sketch and re-initializes it). ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.reset.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.revrank**: Returns, for each floating-point input value, the estimated reverse rank of the value (the number of observations in the sketch that are larger than the value + half the number of observations that are equal to the value) ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.revrank.md) · ★86)
- **tdigest.trimmed_mean**: Returns an estimation of the mean value from the sketch, excluding observation values outside the low and high cutoff quantiles ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.trimmed_mean.md) · ★86)
